0417 — dumbbell standing calf raise

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 0417 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: source_complete_for_storyboard. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: standing.

Holding dumbbells, you rise onto your toes and lower slowly. It builds the calf muscles.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Stand with your feet shoulder-width apart, holding a dumbbell in each hand.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 0417.json#/instructions/0

Arms Hands

Stand with your feet shoulder-width apart, holding a dumbbell in each hand.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 0417.json#/instructions/0

Legs Feet

Stand with your feet shoulder-width apart, holding a dumbbell in each hand.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 0417.json#/instructions/0

Equipment Contacts

Retain every explicit contact in equipment_scene.contacts. Use only the documented prop choices: Dumbbell pair. Do not assign unknown apparatus dimensions, pad settings, attachment heights or load values.

authored_scene_blocking; source verified: false. Scene support follows the source posture; unsupported inventory candidates are excluded from this proposal, not silently asserted. 0417.json#/instructions/0

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Stand with your feet shoulder-width apart, holding a dumbbell in each hand. 0417.json#/instructions/0
  2. Raise your heels off the ground as high as possible, using your calves. 0417.json#/instructions/1
  3. Pause for a moment at the top, then slowly lower your heels back down to the starting position. 0417.json#/instructions/2
  4. Repeat for the desired number of repetitions. 0417.json#/instructions/3

0418 — dumbbell standing concentration curl

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 0418 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: source_complete_for_storyboard. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: standing.

Standing and leaning slightly forward, you curl a dumbbell with your elbow against your inner thigh. It isolates the biceps.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Stand with your feet shoulder-width apart and hold a dumbbell in one hand, with your arm fully extended and palm facing inwards. Place your opposite hand on your thigh for support.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 0418.json#/instructions/0, 0418.json#/instructions/1

Arms Hands

Stand with your feet shoulder-width apart and hold a dumbbell in one hand, with your arm fully extended and palm facing inwards. Place your opposite hand on your thigh for support.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 0418.json#/instructions/0, 0418.json#/instructions/1

Legs Feet

Stand with your feet shoulder-width apart and hold a dumbbell in one hand, with your arm fully extended and palm facing inwards.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 0418.json#/instructions/0

Equipment Contacts

Retain every explicit contact in equipment_scene.contacts. Use only the documented prop choices: Dumbbell pair. For this standing proposal, the support foot or feet contact a level ground plane; that ground plane is an authored scene choice when the source does not name it. Do not assign unknown apparatus dimensions, pad settings, attachment heights or load values.

authored_scene_blocking; source verified: false. Scene support follows the source posture; unsupported inventory candidates are excluded from this proposal, not silently asserted. 0418.json#/instructions/0

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Stand with your feet shoulder-width apart and hold a dumbbell in one hand, with your arm fully extended and palm facing inwards. 0418.json#/instructions/0
  2. Place your opposite hand on your thigh for support. 0418.json#/instructions/1
  3. Keeping your upper arm stationary, exhale and curl the dumbbell towards your shoulder by contracting your biceps. 0418.json#/instructions/2
  4. Continue to raise the dumbbell until your biceps are fully contracted and the dumbbell is at shoulder level. 0418.json#/instructions/3
  5. Hold the contracted position for a brief pause as you squeeze your biceps. 0418.json#/instructions/4
  6. Inhale and slowly lower the dumbbell back to the starting position. 0418.json#/instructions/5
  7. Repeat for the desired number of repetitions, then switch arms. 0418.json#/instructions/6
Extracted position and contact cues

  • Place your opposite hand on your thigh for support. Step 2; 0418.json#/instructions/1

0419 — dumbbell standing front raise above head

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 0419 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: source_complete_for_storyboard. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: standing.

You lift dumbbells in front of you and up above head height. It works the front shoulders and upper chest.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Stand with your feet shoulder-width apart, holding a dumbbell in each hand with an overhand grip.

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Stand with your feet shoulder-width apart, holding a dumbbell in each hand with an overhand grip. 0419.json#/instructions/0
  2. Keep your arms straight and lift the dumbbells in front of you, raising them above your head. 0419.json#/instructions/1
  3. Pause for a moment at the top, then slowly lower the dumbbells back to the starting position. 0419.json#/instructions/2
  4. Repeat for the desired number of repetitions. 0419.json#/instructions/3

0420 — dumbbell standing kickback

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 0420 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: source_complete_for_storyboard. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: standing.

Bent forward, you straighten your arms behind you with dumbbells. It isolates the triceps.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Stand with your feet shoulder-width apart and hold a dumbbell in each hand.

authored_scene_blocking; source verified: false. Scene support follows the source posture; unsupported inventory candidates are excluded from this proposal, not silently asserted. 0420.json#/instructions/0

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Stand with your feet shoulder-width apart and hold a dumbbell in each hand. 0420.json#/instructions/0
  2. Bend your knees slightly and hinge forward at the hips, keeping your back straight. 0420.json#/instructions/1
  3. Extend your arms straight back, squeezing your triceps at the top of the movement. 0420.json#/instructions/2
  4. Pause for a moment, then slowly lower the dumbbells back to the starting position. 0420.json#/instructions/3
  5. Repeat for the desired number of repetitions. 0420.json#/instructions/4

0421 — dumbbell standing one arm concentration curl

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 0421 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: source_complete_for_storyboard. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: supine.

Standing and leaning forward, you curl one dumbbell with your elbow braced on your thigh. It isolates the biceps one side at a time.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

0422 — dumbbell standing one arm curl (over incline bench)

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 0422 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: source_complete_for_storyboard. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: standing.

Standing beside an incline bench, you curl one dumbbell with the upper arm braced on the pad. It keeps the biceps working without swing.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Stand with your feet shoulder-width apart, holding a dumbbell in one hand with your palm facing forward. Place your other hand on an incline bench for support.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 0422.json#/instructions/0, 0422.json#/instructions/1

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Stand upright with your feet shoulder-width apart and hold a dumbbell in each hand, palms facing forward. Rest the back of your upper arms against the preacher bench or an incline bench, with your elbows slightly bent.

  6. Inhale and slowly begin to lower the dumbbells back to the starting position. 0428.json#/instructions/5
  7. Repeat for the desired number of repetitions. 0428.json#/instructions/6

A standing or floor crunch where you bring an elbow toward the opposite knee. It trains the abs and obliques.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Start by lying flat on your back with your knees bent and feet flat on the ground. Place your hands behind your head with your elbows pointing outwards.

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Start by lying flat on your back with your knees bent and feet flat on the ground. 0443.json#/instructions/0
  2. Place your hands behind your head with your elbows pointing outwards. 0443.json#/instructions/1
  3. Engage your abs and lift your upper body off the ground, bringing your right elbow towards your left knee. 0443.json#/instructions/2
  4. At the same time, bring your left knee towards your right elbow, creating a twisting motion. 0443.json#/instructions/3
  5. Pause for a moment at the top, then slowly lower your upper body and extend your legs back to the starting position. 0443.json#/instructions/4
  6. Repeat the movement, this time bringing your left elbow towards your right knee and your right knee towards your left elbow. 0443.json#/instructions/5
  7. Continue alternating sides for the desired number of repetitions. 0443.json#/instructions/6

Source completeness: source_complete_for_storyboard. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: standing.

An advanced kettlebell windmill where you hold the bell overhead and hinge sideways. It trains the obliques, shoulders and hips.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Stand with your feet wider than shoulder-width apart, toes pointing slightly outward. Hold a kettlebell in your right hand, with your arm extended overhead and your palm facing forward.

Hold a kettlebell in your right hand, with your arm extended overhead and your palm facing forward.

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Stand with your feet wider than shoulder-width apart, toes pointing slightly outward. 0517.json#/instructions/0
  2. Hold a kettlebell in your right hand, with your arm extended overhead and your palm facing forward. 0517.json#/instructions/1
  3. Rotate your left foot slightly to the right, and shift your weight onto your left leg. 0517.json#/instructions/2
  4. Bend your left knee and hinge at the hip, lowering your torso towards the left side. 0517.json#/instructions/3
  5. Keep your right arm extended overhead and your eyes on the kettlebell. 0517.json#/instructions/4
  6. As you lower your torso, allow your right leg to straighten and your right foot to pivot slightly. 0517.json#/instructions/5
  7. Lower your torso until you feel a stretch in your left hamstring and your right arm is pointing towards the ground. 0517.json#/instructions/6
  8. Pause for a moment, then engage your core and push through your left heel to return to the starting position. 0517.json#/instructions/7
  9. Repeat for the desired number of repetitions, then switch sides. 0517.json#/instructions/8

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Stand with your feet shoulder-width apart, holding a kettlebell in each hand with an overhand grip. 0518.json#/instructions/0
  2. Bend your knees slightly and hinge forward at the hips, keeping your back straight and chest up. 0518.json#/instructions/1
  3. Allow the kettlebells to hang in front of your body with your arms fully extended. 0518.json#/instructions/2
  4. In one fluid motion, explosively extend your hips, shrug your shoulders, and pull the kettlebells up towards your shoulders. 0518.json#/instructions/3

  6. Lower the kettlebells back down to the starting position and repeat the movement with the opposite arm. 0518.json#/instructions/5
  7. Continue alternating arms for the desired number of repetitions. 0518.json#/instructions/6

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 0524 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: source_complete_for_storyboard. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: standing.

A kettlebell press that takes you from standing to leaning back while the bell stays overhead. It trains the shoulders, trunk and hips.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Stand with your feet shoulder-width apart, holding a kettlebell in your right hand.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 0524.json#/instructions/0

authored_scene_blocking; source verified: false. Scene support follows the source posture; unsupported inventory candidates are excluded from this proposal, not silently asserted. 0524.json#/instructions/0

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Stand with your feet shoulder-width apart, holding a kettlebell in your right hand. 0524.json#/instructions/0
  2. Bend your knees slightly and press the kettlebell overhead with your right arm, keeping your elbow locked. 0524.json#/instructions/1
  3. Rotate your torso to the left, shifting your weight onto your left foot. 0524.json#/instructions/2
  4. Bend your left knee and lower your torso towards the ground, keeping your right arm extended overhead. 0524.json#/instructions/3
  5. As you lower, keep your eyes on the kettlebell and your chest lifted. 0524.json#/instructions/4
  6. Once your left hand touches the ground, push through your left foot and straighten your left leg, driving your hips forward. 0524.json#/instructions/5
  7. As you drive your hips forward, use your core and right arm to press the kettlebell back up to the starting position. 0524.json#/instructions/6
  8. Repeat for the desired number of repetitions, then switch sides. 0524.json#/instructions/7

  2. Bend your knees slightly and hinge forward at the hips, keeping your back straight and chest up. 0526.json#/instructions/1
  3. Allow the kettlebells to hang straight down in front of your body. 0526.json#/instructions/2
